void CID218\+\_\+\+Quantitative\+Image\+Feature::\+cleanup (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}{}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [static]}}

cleanup this context group, i.\+e. delete the internal list of standard coded entries. Usually, there is no need to call this method, but it might be useful in order to explicitly free the associated memory, e.\+g. when checking for memory leaks. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} list will be recreated automatically when needed (or when \doxylink{class_c_i_d218___quantitative_image_feature_aaa44d57c7e0a7c4ccf7456875838d7e0}{initialize()} is called). \Hypertarget{class_c_i_d218___quantitative_image_feature_a011bdf0d5d6598a56e174e44ee5178a9}\index{CID218\_QuantitativeImageFeature@{CID218\_QuantitativeImageFeature}!findCodedEntry@{findCodedEntry}}

\end{DoxyParams}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{class_c_i_d218___quantitative_image_feature_a9ef1d758fe1315bf31e54163e2e89c00} 
\doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_c_i_d218___quantitative_image_feature_a349ed5d7e6f897723f9d7a4d136cfe59}{Code\+List}} \& CID218\+\_\+\+Quantitative\+Image\+Feature::\+get\+Codes (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}{}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [static]}, {\ttfamily [protected]}}

get list of standard coded entries that are managed by this context group. Internally, the singleton pattern is used, so the list is initialized only once. Please note that this function is not thread-\/safe. Therefore, the \doxylink{class_c_i_d218___quantitative_image_feature_aaa44d57c7e0a7c4ccf7456875838d7e0}{initialize()} function can be called before any instance of this class is actually used. \begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
reference to list of coded entries managed by this context group 
\end{DoxyReturn}
\Hypertarget{class_c_i_d218___quantitative_image_feature_aaa44d57c7e0a7c4ccf7456875838d7e0}\index{CID218\_QuantitativeImageFeature@{CID218\_QuantitativeImageFeature}!initialize@{initialize}}
\index{initialize@{initialize}!CID218\_QuantitativeImageFeature@{CID218\_QuantitativeImageFeature}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{initialize()}{initialize()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{class_c_i_d218___quantitative_image_feature_aaa44d57c7e0a7c4ccf7456875838d7e0} 
void CID218\+\_\+\+Quantitative\+Image\+Feature::\+initialize (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}{}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [static]}}

initialize this context group explicitly. Internally, the list of standard coded entries is created and initialized by calling \doxylink{class_c_i_d218___quantitative_image_feature_a9ef1d758fe1315bf31e54163e2e89c00}{get\+Codes()}. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
This function can be called multiple times but in case of multi-\/threaded applications should be called at least once before any instance of this class is actually used. For single-\/threaded applications, there is no need to call it since the initialization is done implicitly. 
\end{DoxyNote}
